## v4.9.239 — Semantic support, structural coherence and phase compatibility are separate measurements; ΔΦ is a tie-breaker (Nic, 12:15 CST)

**Conflict:** 4.9.238 stopped ΔΦ from vetoing a recalled row, but phase still entered support in two places, and through them C. (1) A copula hop was weighed by cos² of its phase gap to the anchor (4.9.235). (2) A world-model premise that recall had not weighed was weighed by G = S·C·(1 + cos ΔΦ)/2 (4.9.236), or by cos² of the gap to the question phase (4.9.185). So a Φ9 memory spoke at C 0.874393 and the same answer from a Φ7 memory at 0.909596. Nic ruled (12:15 CST, as relayed): *"Phase is a retrieval discriminator, not a substitute for semantic evidence."* Semantic support, structural coherence and phase compatibility are separate measurements and must not be folded into C. ΔΦ and phase compatibility are recorded as their own signals on every candidate and every answer, with no veto, and ΔΦ stays a tie-breaker for equally supported candidates (Build A). The 120° separation is a property of the phase model; on its own it does not show that two memories are cognitively incompatible.
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/resonance_recall.py`:
- `_hop_weight_op`: anchor usability × hop rpm, no cos².
- `_phase_signal` (new).
- `_candidate_signals` (new) → report `candidate_signals`.
- `signals` on every active row; `phase_rule` text.

`engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_claim_measure.py`, `_premise_node`:
- An unweighed premise reads 1.0 when the frame gate passed (`derive.gate.S·C`), else its context rpm (`context.rpm`), never G or cos².
- Each premise carries `phase_compatibility`.

`engine/rmc_engine_v1/memory_recaller.py`: the public recall record carries `question_text_phi`, `phase_rule`, `candidate_signals` and `active_signals`.

`engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py`: `_answer_signals` → `result.signals` (forge.answer_signals/v1) on every answer, spoken or held.

`verify/r4239_suite.js` (new). `verify/r4236_suite.js` follows the ruling (premise weight basis `derive.gate.S·C`). Version pins in the r41xx–r42xx suites; registry Partial 196.
**Resolution:** Three measurements are kept apart and none is folded into another:
- **semantic_support:** the recall and premise weights. They are now S·C and context only (μ·e^−ε, 0 when ε > 0.35 or μ ≤ 0).
- **structural_coherence:** the structural Echo checks (kriyā, roles, gap, stated atom) and the frame match.
- **phase_compatibility:** {Φ_identity, Φ_query 7, ΔΦ, A = (1 + cos ΔΦ)/2, and ΔΦ to the anchor for a hop}, with role `tie_breaker`, veto false, in_C false, delta_stored false.

A still orders rows of equal structure in recall and matches of equal S·C in derivation (G), and nothing else. C is the measured kernel formula, unchanged. Two phase *laws* stay where they were, because they are laws rather than ΔΦ compatibility: phase_validity (path legality, 5→8 forbidden) inside C, and the δφ transition term of ε.

Experiment through the real Ask path in headless Chrome (`/workspace/step239/experiment.md`), with 18 questions whose expectations were fixed before the runs:
- **Build A (this release):** 9 true accepts, 4 true rejects, 1 false accept, 4 false rejects.
- **Build B** (A multiplied into every support weight, throwaway branch only): 5 / 4 / 1 / 8. B holds all three sky-colour paraphrases and "Why is the sky blue?" at C 0.845696 / 0.844554. Their structural Echo is 1.0; only support (0.25 = A for the Φ4 NASA row) fell. B rejects nothing that A wrongly accepts.
- **4.9.238:** the same 18 verdicts and replies as A. Five world-model answers move from C 0.874393 to 0.909596, because their premise weight no longer carries A 0.586824.

The colour paraphrases and the cause question retrieve the same memory with the same phase signal (ΔΦ 120°, A 0.25). A tells them apart by frame and asked relation: main clause for the attribute, the whole causal sentence for the cause. Phase cannot tell them apart. The grass questions retrieve a different memory and are held; the sky answer is never accepted. In each paraphrase group all paraphrases retrieved the same memories; the four false rejections are question-frame misses ("author of", "the name of my dog", "whose", plural "whales") and are identical in every build.

Thresholds unchanged (C_min 0.87, Echo 0.85 / 0.97, drift ≤ 0.35 / 0.80, 5→8 forbidden). The R4117 no-model guard is unchanged, no model is called, Wikipedia is not an anchor, and nothing is written to Athena or hosted Supabase.

Open: one false acceptance, common to every build and independent of phase. "Is a living thing a whale?" is answered with "A whale is a living thing." The cause: `derive_frame.match` accepts the swapped polar-copula orientation (kartṛ = fact predicate), which is valid for identity but not for class inclusion with an indefinite predicate, and structural Echo trusts that match for its role checks. The question-frame gaps above are also open, and whether ΔΦ earns any stronger role is left to measurement.

## v4.9.238 — Recall reads the retrieval operation's phase; concessive/causal clauses; "look" links an attribute; structural Echo (Nic, Memory Phase Invariance; plan 229 step 9)

**Conflict:** 4.9.236 moved frame derivation to the operation phase (Φ_query = Φ7), but the recall spine (`resonance_recall.rank_memory`) still kept only rows inside a π/4 window of the question text's phase (plus the 4.9.227 subject-meaning window) and dropped every other row. Nic's rule is that a memory keeps its native Φ_identity, Φ_query is the phase of the cognitive operation (Φ7 for retrieval), not the question text, ΔΦ is temporary and never stored, and G = f(ΔΦ, S, C): phase distance is one factor, never a hard veto, and alignment never makes a match. "What color is the sky?" reads Φ1 from its text, 120° from the NASA row's Φ4, so recall dropped it (4.9.237: C 0.49). That row is also one sentence holding three clauses ("Even though violet is scattered more than blue, the sky looks blue to us because our eyes are more sensitive to blue light."), so no copula fact "the sky looks blue" was ever observed, and "look" was not read as a linking verb. Plan step 9 asks that Echo score the meaning structure of the spoken clause against the question frame, not word overlap.
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/resonance_recall.py` (`_operation_grace`, `_hop_weight_op`; `rank_memory`: Φ_query from `derive_frame.OPERATION_PHASE["retrieve"]`, `question_text_phi` only tunes the band, the 4.9.227 second window removed, concept key `specific` before grace, only an A.* concept hit displaces a role lock, evidence `question_text_phi`/`operation`/`delta_phi_deg`/`A`, report `phase_rule`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/clause_split.py` (new) and `src/qframe.js` (`splitClauses`, `clauseSplit`; same rules), `src/rmc_core.js` (a split clause row counts when any part parsed), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/memory_clauses.py` (indexes split parts), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`clause_units`; facts carry `clause_link` and the parent `sentence`; public claim carries `atom` and `fact_frame`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/derive_frame.py` (`LINKING_VERBS = {"look"}`, `is_value_of`, `attribute_of`, `_first_person_roles`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/structural_echo.py` (new), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (structural Echo for a world-model answer; `_trust_trace`: a reply that is one clause of a trusted row rests on it), `verify/r4238_suite.js` (new); suites that asserted the retired text-phase rule follow it: `verify/r4218_suite.js` (tuned-phase test: Φ_query 7, text Φ1 recorded; t-centre wins on ΔΦ, nothing dropped), `verify/r4219_suite.js` (band test: b-z nearer Φ7 wins; the far-phase b-h row is admitted by its role lock, not vetoed), `verify/r4209_suite.js` and `verify/r4217_suite.js` (concept key order), version pins in the r41xx–r42xx suites.
**Resolution:** Recall is the operation *retrieve*: Φ_query = Φ7 for every row, whatever phase the question text reads (that reading is kept as `question_text_phi` and still tunes the symbolic band). Each row keeps its own packet phase; ΔΦ = Φ7 − Φ_identity is computed per ask (`delta_stored: false`, `identity_rewritten: false`) and gives A = (1 + cos ΔΦ)/2, the same factor `derive_frame.gate` uses. As in that gate, S (a frame-role lock or a named concept) and C (a stated ε ≤ 0.35 and μ > 0) decide; A only orders rows of equal structure. A row is dropped only for context, never for phase, and a usable anchor's support reads 1.0 as an in-window anchor did before; copula hops keep their 4.9.235 weight against the anchor's phase. Because A to Φ7 no longer says which row a question is about, a concept hit's named subject (an A.* concept) now ranks before A, and only such a hit can displace a role lock: without this a trusted leaves sentence sharing only "color" outranked the user's "My car is red.", and a row nearer Φ7 outranked the sky anchors. A sentence joined by *even though / although / though* (concessive) or *because* (hetu, not "because of") is split into clauses by the same rules on the phone and in the engine, and each clause is observed as its own fact with its link and parent sentence. "look" is read as the copula only for an attribute question ("What color is X?", gap pred of kind K; "What is the K of X?"), and only when the complement is a value of K by the concept lexicon (blue is a colour); a kind with no value rule (breed) is not an attribute, so "What breed is my dog?" still holds. A memory is personal only when a kāraka role is first person; the experiencer adjunct "to us" is not. For a world-model answer Echo is now structural (`structural_frame`, `uses_token_overlap: false`): same kriyā (or the linking or replayed relational rule), every role the question knows held by the fact, the gap filled, and the atom stated in the spoken clause; the 4.9.217 coverage score is logged beside it as `replaced`. Real asks through the Ask path in headless Chrome (`/workspace/step238/asks.txt`): "What is the color of the sky?" and "What color is the sky?" both speak "The sky looks blue to us." (C 0.909596, Echo 1.0 structural, ε 0.006667; the NASA row at Φ4 is 120° from Φ7, A 0.25, recorded, not a veto); whale chain C 0.874393 Echo 1; Hamlet and oxygen (quoted) C 0.909596 Echo 1; dog's name and "who owns Rex" (answered "You own Rex.") C 0.874393 Echo 1; breed and age held; "What is a whale?" asks which sense. "Why is the sky blue?" still speaks the whole NASA sentence (C 0.909596). Thresholds unchanged (C_min 0.87, Echo 0.85 to speak and 0.97 to write, drift ≤ 0.35 with the 0.80 hard stop, 5→8 forbidden), the R4117 no-model guard unchanged, no model, Wikipedia is not an anchor, nothing written to Athena or hosted Supabase. Open: structural Echo covers world-model answers only (anchor-only answers keep the 4.9.217 re-parse, speech-act Echo is still forced to 1.0); "look" is the only linking verb; the phone renderer's `echoOf` still measures a changed R_t by coverage; `language_core_grok_infer` still reads the π/4 grace window; age has no frame.

## v4.9.237 — The output is written by panini_wording's outward compile (Nic, plan 229 step 8)

**Conflict:** Through 4.9.236 the phone showed the engine's stored or derived string as it was: the Pāṇini wording module only re-read an admitted sentence as a tag (4.9.217), so no admitted answer was ever written by the grammar. Plan step 8 asks for the derived atom or clause to be rendered by panini_wording into R_t, for the live Ask to speak R_t instead of the stored string, for a round trip (reparseClause(R_t) gives back the derived atom), and for the source sentence to stay as a cited quote.
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`_listener_atom`, `_spoken_atoms`; claim `spoken_atoms`; report and seam `derived_atoms`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`_outward_plan`; result `outward`, forge.outward/v1), `src/panini_wording.js` (`outward`, `atomOfClause`), `src/kernel.js` (`forge.outward`), `src/rmc_core.js` (`outwardCompile` after `RMC.ask`; OUTWARD-COMPILE-FAIL, OUTWARD-ECHO-FAIL, OUTWARD-COMPILE-ERROR), `src/rmc_renderer_200.js` (exports `echoOf`), `src/app.js` (data-outward tag, data-source-quote quotes, data-answer-source panini_wording.outward), `verify/r4237_suite.js`.
**Resolution:** The engine still decides and measures; it now sends each spoken sentence with the atom it states (premises in the listener's person, the conclusion as the rule derived it), only when the reply is the world model's derived text. On the phone each atom-bearing sentence is read as the kernel's own sentence into a kāraka clause, which must state the engine's atom (same kriyā, kartṛ, karma or predicate nominal, polarity); grammar.js realizes the clause (clauseText, the kernel speaking) into R_t; R_t is re-read and must give the same material clause. The joined R_t is what the card shows; the stored premises are kept beside it as cited quotes. A sentence the engine sent without an atom, or a recalled sentence spoken verbatim that the phone grammar cannot read at all (oxygen's bracketed atomic weight), is spoken as the cited quote it is; a sentence that reads as another atom, or a rule-made sentence that does not read, holds the answer; a realized surface that differs from the measured one is Echo-measured again with the renderer's own echoOf. Today R_t equals the measured text for every derived answer (the realizer re-states the clause), so Echo is unchanged. Limits: only world-model derived answers carry atoms (anchor answers such as oxygen are quoted); the grammar does not yet choose a different wording. Thresholds unchanged (drift 0.35 / 0.80, C_min 0.87, Echo 0.85 / 0.97); no model. Sky (investigated, not changed): "What is the color of the sky?" recalls one trusted sentence ("Even though violet is scattered more than blue, the sky looks blue to us …") through its subject meaning (Φ4), but no copula answer survives the debate in it, so the rendered text is empty and Echo reads 0 on nothing. "What color is the sky?" has no subject meaning, so the question text's Φ1 is 120° from that row's Φ4 and recall drops it (C 0.49): the recall spine still gates on the question text's phase, which the 4.9.236 rule retired only for frame derivation.

## v4.9.236 — Memory Phase Invariance with Contextual Phase Alignment: the operation, not the question, is phased (Nic, 10:39 CST)

**Conflict:** 4.9.233 answered a frame match only when its placement was within δ ≤ π/4 of the question text's phase, and weighed it by grace = rpm·cos²δ. "My dog is Rex." sits at Φ9; "What is my dog's name?" reads Φ7 from its text, 80° away, so the one memory that answers it was vetoed, and "Who owns Rex?" and "What breed is my dog?" had no frame route at all. Nic ruled (10:39 CST, as relayed in the brief): *"Memory Phase Invariance with Contextual Phase Alignment. A memory keeps Φ_identity, its native content phase; never rewrite it to fit a query. Φ_query is the phase of the cognitive operation (for example, Φ7 for retrieval), not of the question text. ΔΦ = Φ_query − Φ_identity is a temporary relational value and is never stored. The gate is G = f(ΔΦ, S, C), with S = semantic similarity (frame match) and C = contextual coherence; ΔΦ is one factor, not a hard veto at δ ≤ 45°. Phase alignment can never create a match: content has to independently pass the semantic and contextual checks. Model the operation, not the question, so name, breed, age and 'who owns Rex' can all retrieve the same memory."*
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/derive_frame.py` (`OPERATION_PHASE`, `operation`, `phase_factor`, `context_coherence`, `gate`, `possessor`, `owner_of`, kind check on a copula gap), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`OWNER_RULE`, `NAME_RULE`, `_replay_owner`, `_replay_name`, `_rule_words`, `to_listener`, gate-sorted frame derivation), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/language_core_debate.py` (a replayed rule licenses only its own head words), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_claim_measure.py` (premise weight G, measured and routed in the memory's own phase and person), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`_trust_trace`: a personal row spoken to the listener still rests on that row), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/relation_echo.py`, `engine/rmc_engine_v1/echo_question.py`, and their copies `src/relation_echo.js` and `src/rmc_renderer_200.js` (agreement on the asked verb only: "own" realises and covers "owns"; "lightbulbs" is still not folded into "lightbulb"), `src/qframe.js` (`QF-WH-DET-COP-SUBJ`: "what breed is my dog"), `verify/r4236_suite.js` (new), `verify/r4233_suite.js` (tests 1, 3, 4 follow the ruling).
**Resolution:** Every frame derivation is the operation *retrieve*, Φ_query = Φ7 (codex v2.5 Naming), whatever the question text reads. Each matched claim carries `gate` = {operation, Φ_query, Φ_identity, ΔΦ, A = (1 + cos ΔΦ)/2, S, C, G = S·C·A, passes = S ∧ C, delta_stored: false, identity_rewritten: false}. S is the frame unification (no match, no claim; A can never make one). C is 1 only when the proof replayed, the debate survived, no premise is conflicted or quarantined, and every first-person premise is personal (source `phone_kb`). ΔΦ only ranks matches and weighs the premise (G, basis `derive.gate.G`) where recall left no weight; Φ9 → Φ7 is 80°, A 0.586824. The memory is measured in its own phase tags (`memory.phase_identity`), not the question path. Two relational rules are replayed by the checker like WM-COPULA-CHAIN: WM-NAME-OF (is(X, N), N a proper name ⊢ is(X's name, N)) and WM-OWNER-OF (is(my X, N) ⊢ owns(I, N)); a forged filler or a common-noun "name" fails replay. A personal answer is spoken to the listener ("Your dog is Rex. Your dog's name is Rex." / "Your dog is Rex. You own Rex.") and measured as stored; the trust trace reads a phone_kb row in both persons, so the reply still rests on it. The router reads the spoken memory's own phase path too, so "What is my dog's name?" (text Φ7) is not sent to the correction queue for "Φ7 naming without Φ6": the Φ7 is the operation's, not the content's. A copula question with a kind ("what breed") needs a filler whose head is that kind, so with no breed stored it is undetermined and holds. Thresholds unchanged (C_min 0.87, Echo 0.85/0.97, drift ≤ 0.35 / 0.80), no model, Wikipedia is not an anchor, nothing is written. Open: retrieval is the only operation with a mapped phase; age ("how old is my dog") has no frame yet and holds; "my dog is a beagle" + "a beagle is a breed" is not resolved by the kind check.

## v4.9.235 — Measured manifest values and per-sentence drift; the meaning is asked before the engine (Nic, plan 229 step 7)

**Conflict:** Every language-core sentence read D 0.331794 and ε 0.110598, whatever it said: D's entropy term was the candidate's no-information entropy (about 1.0 for any short sentence) and its structure term a dict-shape delta fixed per candidate class. The live manifest (μ_t) carried fixed numbers (confidence 0.90, novelty 0.20, ε 0.20, memory fit 0.80, source confidence 0.80); `scoring_candidate` carried fixed phase, confidence and drift; the world model gave every premise retrieval weight 0.8 and confidence "medium_high"; recall gave every copula hop weight 0.72. Separately, 4.9.229 asked "Which whale do you mean?" after the engine had already answered. The quartz pins in r4184/r4185/r4186 ("a short chain stays under C_min") held only because of the constant drift (ε 0.144, C 0.858).
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/measurement_kernel.py` (new `relative_entropy`, `sentence_form`, `form_delta`; `memory_relation` returns `support_texts`; `measure_candidate` adds `D_terms`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/deep_dry_run_orchestrator.py` (`_manifest_reading`, `_live_manifest(..., reading)`, measured novelty on the language-core candidate), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/language_core_orchestration.py` (`scoring_candidate(chain, hop)`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`_premise_weight`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/resonance_recall.py` (`_hop_weight`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_claim_measure.py` (drift terms), `src/app.js` (sense ask before `KRMCCore.run`), `verify/r4235_suite.js` (new), `verify/r4229_suite.js`, `verify/r4184_suite.js`, `verify/r4185_suite.js`, `verify/r4186_suite.js`.
**Resolution:** With recalled nodes, D's entropy is relative to them (H_norm of the claim × the share of its −log2 p bits whose tokens the nodes do not carry) and its structure is the sentence-form distance (function words kept, content runs slotted, 1–3-gram Jaccard) to the nearest node. With no nodes the old terms stand. The D formula and weights are unchanged. A sentence identical to its memory now reads ε ≈ 0.007; the derived conclusion "A whale is a living thing." reads ε 0.044. The manifest carries the measured reading: the world model's weakest hop (C, memory fit, source confidence) and the largest ε and novelty over the spoken sentences, or else the orchestrator's selected candidate. `scoring_candidate` reads the hop's Φ, grace and ε (0 when absent, never a made-up value). Premise weight is recall's own weight, or else the resonance grace at the question's phase, or else 0; confidence is the row's own or "medium". A copula hop weighs the anchor's weight × the hop's grace at the anchor's phase. The meaning question is planned from the question and the sense data only, before the engine runs; the held path asks only the unit question, which needs the engine's reading. The quartz pins now require the unchanged gate: a stored sentence admitted at C ≥ 0.87, ε ≤ 0.35 and Echo ≥ 0.85, or held. Real Ask run (headless Chrome, local 4.9.235): "Is a whale a living thing?" is spoken from the world-model chain at C 0.874393, ε 0.044111 (4.9.234 held it at C 0.854). "What is a whale?" asks "Which whale" with no engine run; replying "2" answers "A whale is a mammal." at C 0.909596. Hamlet is spoken at C 0.909596 and ε 0.006667, as are Paris and oxygen. The sky is held on an Echo fail. The dog question is held at C 0.436521; the phase decision is Nic's and untouched. Thresholds unchanged: drift 0.35 with a stop at 0.80, C_min 0.87, Echo 0.85 to speak and 0.97 to write. No model.

## v4.9.234 — World model in series; the Ask gate scores the chain it speaks (Nic, plan 229 step 6)

**Conflict:** RMC and the world model ran side by side: the Ask gate (C, Echo, drift) scored the orchestrator's own candidate, so a world-model chain could be spoken on another sentence's reading, or held on it — live 4.9.233, "Is a whale a living thing?" derived the whale chain but was held on the orchestrator's correction_queue reading (C 0.486, ε 0.186), which is not the sentence the world model would speak. The RMC reading never reached the world model, and observe rows carried no recall weight, so every premise read weight 0 (C ≈ 0.80 for every chain).
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_claim_measure.py` (new: `upstream_reading`, `measure_spoken`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/deep_dry_run_orchestrator.py` (upstream passed into the reasoning seam; `artifacts.world_model_measure`; late route = the world-model route), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (returns `upstream`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`_measured_from_world`; gate `scored`/`scored_reading`/`orchestrator_reading`; `world_model_measure` in trace and result), `data/feature_registry.json` (R4234), `verify/r4234_suite.js`, `verify/r4217_suite.js` and `verify/r4218_suite.js` (CONFLICTS allowance +1 each for this entry), bundle + SRI.
**Resolution:** Series, not parallel: RMC's reading (C, ε, route, phase path) is handed to the world model as `upstream` and kept in the trace. Each sentence the world model speaks is measured with the same kernel as every other candidate (`measurement_kernel.measure_candidate` + `coherence_components`, weights unchanged) against its own recalled premise row; the premise weight is recall's measured `retrieval_weight` from `memory_recall.active_memory_nodes` (the highest-weight row with the same text wins, so a duplicate hosted row cannot displace the recall-locked anchor); a premise with no recall weight and no packet gets `alignment.grace`, else 0. `route_candidate` routes the result. When the sentence the turn would speak equals the world model's spoken text (a dry run uses its candidate text, so dry and live score the same), the Ask gate scores that reading (`gate.scored = "world_model"`); otherwise the orchestrator reading stays (`"orchestrator"`). C rule (my interpretation, for Nic to confirm): C = min over spoken hops; the derived conclusion is checked by checker replay and ε, its lexical C (≈ 0.76) is reported, not gated. ε = max over every spoken sentence. Thresholds unchanged: C_min 0.87, drift ≤ 0.35 / hard stop 0.80, Echo 0.85/0.97; R4117 guard unchanged. Real Ask (headless Chrome, local tree): "Is a whale a living thing?" is now scored on its own chain — C 0.854 (weakest hop "A mammal is an animal.", premise grace 0.587, facts at Φ9 vs question Φ1), ε 0.155, route active_stack_candidate — below 0.87, so it is held honestly; no threshold was loosened to speak it. "Who wrote Hamlet?" (C 0.889, ε 0.120), "What is oxygen?" (C 0.890, ε 0.117) and "What is a whale?" (C 0.890, ε 0.117) are scored world_model and pass; capital of France, sky and dog's name derive nothing from memory and stay on the orchestrator reading. The constant language-core drift (D 0.3318 → ε 0.1106) and the sense question asked after the engine admits are left for 4.9.235 (step 7). Dog-name phase decision untouched (Nic deciding).

## v4.9.233 — Derive by frame match and phase/band alignment (Nic, plan 229 step 5; 9:27 steering)

**Conflict:** Derive ranked facts by question-word coverage and role lock, so the spoken survivor was whichever sentence shared the most words: live, the world model derived "a whale is a living thing" but spoke the "…a mammal is a living thing" chain, and "What is oxygen?" could pick a photosynthesis sentence that only shares a token. The question frame (4.9.230) and every fact's frame and placement (4.9.231–232) reached Derive but were not used, and no phase, band or frequency code took part in the choice (Nic 9:27: match on frame AND phase/band alignment, not on frame alone).
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/derive_frame.py` (new: `match`, `np_unify`, `fact_frame`, `chain_frame`, `alignment`, rule WM-NAME-OF), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`derive(..., qframe)`, `_derive_by_frame`, `_frame_claim`, `_frame_key`, `role_frame` and the `role_frame` column, `_derive_report(answers=…)`, public claims carry `frame_match`/`alignment`/`coverage_tiebreak`; `run_world_model` reads `question_frame.current()` and notes `world_model.derive`), `data/feature_registry.json` (R4233), `verify/r4233_suite.js`, `verify/r4230_suite.js` (its speech-act check now skips the new `world_model.derive` use note), bundle + SRI.
**Resolution:** General machinery, no per-question cases. A fact answers only when (1) its frame unifies with the question frame — same kriyā; every known role of the question has the same head (plural-normalised), its words inside the fact's phrase and the same owner (a personal "my" role never matches a general fact); the gap role is filled — and (2) its placement is phase-aligned with a phase on the question's phase path: `resonance_recall._grace` (grace = rpm·cos²δ, δ ≤ π/4, ε/μ in band). Aligned matches are ranked by grace, `band_tuning.relation` (same/x3/x9/none) and the 4.9.204 coupling ζ·cos²δ·e^(−β|Δh|/marker) from `frequency_204.attract_dh` between the fact's Hz and the question tuned on that phase; fewer premises, then word coverage, are logged tie-breaks only. Copula: "What is X?" reads X as the fact's kartṛ; a definite X ("the capital of France") may also be the fact's predicate (specificational), an indefinite one may not ("What is a mammal?" is not answered by "A whale is a mammal."). A polar question needs both roles and records yes/no. One relational-noun rule, WM-NAME-OF: "X's name" is the proper-noun predicate of a copula fact on X. A fact frame is its clause frame, else the roles of a clause that did not cover the sentence (marked partial), else its is() atom. A copula-chain conclusion carries the frame built from its premises; non-matching conclusions stay as hops only; the survivor speaks its own premises and conclusion. No frame match → undetermined ("no fact frame unifies with the question frame"); a match that is not aligned → undetermined ("frame matched but no match is phase-aligned"). Without a question frame (engine-only callers) the old coverage ranking runs, labelled by the absent `match_mode`. Live local Ask run (headless Chrome, after Remember whale→mammal→animal→living thing, Shakespeare wrote Hamlet, my dog is Rex): the world model now picks "a whale is a living thing." (polar, yes; chain spoken in full), "A whale is a mammal." for "What is a whale?", "Shakespeare wrote Hamlet." (spoken), and the oxygen definition, never the photosynthesis sentence. Honest limits: remembered facts sit at the placement default Φ9 with no meaning id, so a Φ1 question aligns at δ 40° (grace 0.587, band relation none, coupling 0), and "What is my dog's name?" frame-matches "My dog is Rex." by WM-NAME-OF but is held by the phase gate (question Φ7, fact Φ9, δ 80°) — not loosened; the Ask gate still measures the orchestrator's candidate (C 0.486 for the whale polar question), so a correct world-model claim is still held. No model; no write; thresholds unchanged.

## v4.9.232 — world-model facts from clauses, each placed in phase, band and frequency (Nic, plan 229 step 4; 9:27 steering)

**Conflict:** 4.9.231 brought each recalled row's clause and placement to Observe, but Observe still built every atom from a regex over six closed verbs ("is", "is not", "owns", …) and dropped the clause and the placement, so no world fact carried a meaning id, phase, band or frequency, and a transitive statement ("Shakespeare wrote Hamlet") was only ever "stated".
**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/world_model_v1.py` (`atom_from_clause`, `clause_record`, `_np_frame`, `_place_fields`, `_derived_place`; `observe(..., regex_fallback=True)` with `atom_sources` and `clause_fallback_reasons`; `_fact_row` frame/atom_source/place; self facts placed; `run_world_model` note `world_model.observe_atoms`, world facts list placement), `data/feature_registry.json` (R4232; R4231 note corrected: no hosted migration), `verify/r4232_suite.js`, bundle + SRI.
**Resolution:** General machinery, no per-question cases. A sentence's atom comes from its clause: kriyā be with a pred → is(kartṛ, pred) keeping the clause's polarity; any other verb with a karma → lemma(kartṛ, karma). The clause is used only when its roles cover every content word of the sentence (the verb's surface form between kartṛ and karma excepted); a parse that dropped words — "Water is made of hydrogen and oxygen" parses as the adjective "made" — is recorded `clause_incomplete` and the old regex reading is used instead, so the clause never makes a fact poorer than the text. Every fact row carries its frame (kartṛ/pred/karma with head, owner, of, propn), `atom_source` (clause / regex / stated / self_fact / derive), its place, and top-level meaning_id, phase, phase_name, band, hz, group; a derived row sits at its subject premise and keeps `premise_phases` and `premise_meaning_ids`. With the regex switched off, three clause facts ("A whale is a mammal. A mammal is an animal. An animal is a living thing.") alone derive and check "a whale is a living thing" in two hops. Honest limit: Derive still ranks direct facts by token coverage and speaks the top survivor's chain plus siblings, so the asked hop is not always the one spoken; frame and phase alignment in Derive is step 5. The world root now digests the frame and placement too. No model; no write; thresholds unchanged.

## v4.9.231 — memory as clauses, each placed in phase, band and frequency (Nic, plan 229 step 3; 9:27 steering)

**Conflict:** Memory rows reached the engine as bare text; the world model's Observe re-read each sentence with regexes and knew nothing of its grammar, and it never saw the row's meaning id or its resonance placement: the orchestrator dropped the packet (Phi, band, Hz), the meaning id and the clause on the way to `run_reasoning_seam`, so no world fact carried a phase, band or frequency (Nic 9:27: every clause and world fact must carry meaning id, phase Φ1–9, band and frequency).
**Where:** `src/qframe.js` (`KQFrame.clause`, `clauseCached`, schema `forge.clause/v1`; NP owner/of/adjs/det/plural), `src/rmc_core.js` (`withClauses` in `KRMCCore.run`), `src/app.js` (remembered statement keeps its clause; phone rows forward it), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/memory_clauses.py` (new), `rmc_core.py` (`run` holds the turn's clauses; receipts carry clause and meaning_id; `result.memory_clauses`), `memory_recaller.py` (receipt node keeps clause and meaning_id), `deep_dry_run_orchestrator.py` (Observe nodes keep clause, meaning_id, packet, concepts, sfe), `resonance_recall.place_packet` (`phi_basis`), `world_model_v1.place_row` + Observe events, `verify/r4231_suite.js`, bundle + SRI.
**Resolution:** General machinery, no per-question cases. The phone parses every memory row it sends with its own grammar (one parse per text, cached; 6,699 rows in about 5 ms on a warm cache); a row that does not parse is sent without a clause, a stored clause is kept, the row's meaning_id rides on its clause. The engine accepts a clause only when its schema is right and it was parsed from that row's own text. Observe attaches to each event its clause and `place_row`: meaning id, Phi, phase name, Codex band (drift_policy BAND_TABLE via band_tuning.tune), symbolic Hz, the 144/432/1296 group (frequency_203_config.HOME), eps, mu and `phi_basis` (lexicon_resonance / row_sfe / default_projectable / default_quarantine), from the resonance packet recall already placed. The parser's copula role is kept as parsed: "Paris is the capital of France" is kriyā be, kartṛ Paris, pred "the capital of France" with of France, karma none; it is not relabelled. Clauses are not yet used to build atoms (step 4) or to derive (step 5). Honest limits seen live: most rows' phase is the placement default (Φ9 projectable for phone rows, Φ5 for idle anchors), no lexicon placement fired for these asks, and only rows filed under a meaning carry a meaning id (1 of 52 for gravity). The hosted write is unchanged: the clause is kept on the phone only; the phone re-parses hosted and anchor rows at every Ask, so no hosted column or migration is needed. No model; no write; thresholds unchanged.

## v4.9.230 — question → kāraka frame reaches the engine (Nic, plan 229 steps 1–2)

**Conflict:** The Ask path parsed questions in JS (KGrammar) but sent only the raw text to Python; `language_core_proto_chat._speech_act` re-guessed the speech act from keywords, so "Who wrote Hamlet?" and the bottle conditional were read as `stated`.
**Where:** `src/qframe.js` (new), `src/rmc_core.js` (`KRMCCore.run` attaches the frame to every question payload: Ask, the sense re-ask, the Dry-Run card), `index.html`, `sw.js`, `engine/rmc_engine_v1/question_frame.py` (new), `rmc_core.run`, `language_core_proto_chat.handle_chat`, `data/py/rmc_engine_bundle.json`, `src/rmc_client.js` SRI.
**Resolution:** General machinery, no per-question cases: `KQFrame.compile` fills the wh-gap with an indefinite (what→something, who→someone; why/how/where/when → hetu/prakāra/adhikaraṇa/kāla adjuncts), undoes do-support, and parses the result with the existing declarative grammar into kartṛ/karma/pred/kriyā/tense/polarity/condition (schema `forge.qframe/v1`, `calls_llm:false`). The frame rides in the payload; `rmc_core.run` validates it (schema + text must equal the question) and holds it in a ContextVar for the turn; `handle_chat` uses the frame's speech act when the frame parsed (or is partial with a kartṛ) and the act is one the engine knows, otherwise keeps the heuristic. Every use is recorded in `result.qframe.uses` with the heuristic it replaced. Live local Ask run: frames valid on all 15 engine asks; Hamlet and the bottle conditional now `qframe:who/what` (heuristic said `stated`); arithmetic/convert/hello keep the heuristic. Thresholds and the R4117 no-model guard unchanged.

## v4.9.220 — Athena places each fact she writes: decimal phase from content, band from phase (Nic 2026-11-04, t132u "Ship it and do that")

**Conflict:** through 4.9.219 Athena stamped every projectable promote Φ9 (the gate's standing phase) and self-seed anchors carried only the integer lexicon phase, so stored facts had no band and no decimal; the band existed only at read time.

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/band_tuning.py` (place), `self_seed_202.py` (anchor packet band + placement), `src/band_tuning.js` (new JS port, Python-round exact), `src/self_seed_202.js`, `src/dream_idle.js` (factPlacement on the promote and hosted item), `src/kpacks.js` (lawfulPlacement), `index.html`, `sw.js`, `tools/check_220.py`, `tools/athena_dryrun_220.py`, `tools/parity_220_pyodide.js`, `verify/r4220_suite.js`; `verify/r4217_suite.js`/`r4218_suite.js` (CONFLICTS position bodies; names kept).

**Resolution:**
- **Integer phase:** the fact's content placement already used by the self-seed gate (lexicon concept packet, else letter resultant). No per-question table.
- **Band:** from that phase alone (D15 table).
- **Decimal:** Φ = band + (hz − low)/(high − low), the fact's symbolic Hz position in its band; band_of(Φ) returns the band.
- **Stored alongside, gates untouched:** packet.band and packet.placement; sfe.Phi (gate phase) unchanged; ε_s ≤ 0.35, Echo 0.97 memory writes, server ε recompute, Ask ε /3 all unchanged.
- **Parity:** JS = CPython on all 3101 live anchor texts (0 mismatches); Pyodide = CPython.
- **No bulk fill, no live write.** Dry-run on real gaps (Moon, Burkina Faso, its capital, gravity, Mercury): no admissible source found (LOC catalog records only, Wikipedia pointer only, NASA APOD sentences fail shape), so nothing would be written.

**Open (Nic):** decimal convention (sector-start Φ = band + fraction, typical Φ1 fact ≈ 1.5, 20° from an integer Φ1 question) vs sector-centre; whether stored sfe.Phi should become the content phase instead of Φ9 standing (affects grace-ancestor alignment).

Centres are 144/n divisors; no overlaps except Φ6.

## v4.9.218 — Continuous question tuning on the 9 / 144 dial; frequency constants approved (Nic approval 2026-11-01 8:06 AM America/Chicago, pt125u "Build that")

**Conflict:** through 4.9.217 the question read one integer phase, so its tuning was coarse: every packet in the same band got the same gap and grace, while RSOC Axiom 2 treats Φ as a real number. The 4.9.203 / 4.9.204 frequency constants (ε 0.02 on hz/marker, offset cap 0.40, nudge span 0.45) were marked "proposal v1, awaiting Nic".

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/question_tuning.py` (new), `resonance_recall.py` (rank_memory), `memory_recaller.py` (record), `drift_policy.py` (QUESTION_TUNING, D14 note), `frequency_203_config.py` (approval note), `src/app.js` (Recall line), `src/rmc_client.js` (bundle SRI), `tools/check_218.py`, `tools/parity_218_pyodide.js`, `verify/r4218_suite.js`.

**Resolution:**
- **Band:** the integer phase the engine already reads (the asked concept's lexicon placement, else the parser's top phase). One band is 40° (2π/9; 144/9 = 16 Hz). The band never changes.
- **Decimal:** the question's content words each carry their 4.9.203 signature. The tuning is the amplitude-weighted mean place r of those components inside their Appendix A marker cells (|r| ≤ 0.40, a surface nudge ≤ 0.45·ε, ε = 0.02), placed on the dial as offset = r · ½ band. So |offset| ≤ 0.20 phase (±8°). Φ1 − δ is written Φ9.x (the same point on the circle).
- **Lookup:** rank_memory's π/4 window and grace = rpm · cos²δ read the continuous gap between the tuned question and each packet. The record carries question_band, question_phi (tuned) and question_tuning.
- **Constants approved unchanged:** ε 0.02, offset cap 0.40, nudge 0.45 (Nic, pt125u). Recorded in drift_policy.QUESTION_TUNING, frequency_203_config.CONSTANTS_APPROVED and DRIFT-SPEC/v1 D14. They do not enter either ε. The other 4.9.203 / 4.9.204 proposal items (RSOC operator coefficients, the 4.9.204 field) are not changed.
- **General:** a pure function of the question's letters and its band; no question list. CPython and Pyodide (shipped bundle) agree exactly.
- **Kept:** Ask's candidate ε /3; the 4.9.217 decimal packet phase; definition-first rank; relation Echo; 0.85 / 0.97; the math, remembered-fact and Dry-Run paths (Dry-Run output identical to 4.9.217 on 63 questions).
- **Effect today:** every stored packet is an integer phase (Φ1, Φ4, Φ5, Φ9) and the offset stays inside the band, so no lock changed: the cold 60 is recorded, not tuned, 21/60, 0 false admits, every row identical to 4.9.217. The tuning starts to separate anchors when packets carry decimal phases (a fixture shows the nearest station winning on grace where an integer question preferred the band centre).

## v4.9.217 — Lookup ranks definitions; Echo re-parses the asked relation; /3 is canonical (Nic request 2026-11-01 7:08 AM America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** "What is the Moon?" was admitted on a sentence that only mentions the Moon ("The Moon, on the other hand, moves completely around Earth"). "What is DNA?" spoke "Most DNA is located …" when a definition was in the store. Echo passed both because it counted only words. DRIFT-SPEC/v1 D3 gave N = max(1, depth) as if it applied to every ε, while the Ask candidate ε divides by 3.

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/relation_echo.py` (new), `resonance_recall.py`, `rmc_core.py` (preselect_pools), `echo_question.py`, `src/relation_echo.js` (new; the renderer card's copy), `src/rmc_renderer_200.js`, `src/app.js`, `index.html`, `sw.js`, `tools/check_217.py`, `verify/r4217_suite.js`; comments only in `drift_policy.py`, `mobile_config.py`, `drift_engine.py`, `src/kmath.js`.

**Resolution:**
- **Lookup:** a row that DEFINES the asked subject ranks right after trust, in rank_memory and in preselect_pools.
  - Rank 2: the subject is the kartṛ of FR.BE.COP with a noun-phrase or naming predicate, or an appositive. For an identity question, the asked phrase sits on a side of the copula.
  - Rank 1: a copular attribution.
  - Rank 0: a mention.
  - The rule is general: there is no question list and no token overlap.
- **Echo:** the answer is re-parsed with the engine's copular parse and the Pāṇinian kāraka readings. It must realise the asked relation (definition, identify, attribute, cause, quantity, location, time, agent, manner) about the asked subject, or the score is capped at 0.5.
  - Coverage stays the secondary number. 0.85 to speak and 0.97 to write are unchanged.
  - The math Echo (computed_quantity_reparse) and the remembered-fact Echo ("My car is red.") are unchanged.
  - The card shows the relation, plus the Pāṇini wording module's re-parse of the admitted sentence as evidence only.
- **/3 is canonical** for the Ask candidate ε = (σ_res + D_score + ∣ΔΦ∣)/3. Nic 2026-11-01: the nine-phase cycle on the 144 base runs by threes (144 = 9×16 = 3×48; three octaves of three phases; one triad step = 3/9; ω₀/3ω₀/9ω₀). RMC §6.3 sets n = 3 for the three components. max(1, depth) is the write-trace normaliser only (RSOC v4.8 Eq. 5; Addendum). No value changed.
- **Decimal phase:** resonance_recall keeps a real Φ on the 40° dial (RSOC Axiom 2) instead of flooring it. Integer packets read as before. Continuous question tuning is proposed as 4.9.218.
- **Tests kept, assertions updated** (names unchanged):
  - R4209: the fallback is now exercised by a cause question, because the planet question is answered first-ranked; the rank regex includes `_defines`.
  - R4210: the Moon pick is checked on the re-parsed sentence, which is now held.
  - R4215: the c4_retired_tests.json mention is looked up in the 4.9.215 entry, not at a fixed offset.
- **Cold 60** is recorded, not tuned: 21/60, 0 false admits. It was 27/60. DNA now gives the definition, and helium, magnet, atom, lightning, Moon and Nile are held because their anchors only mention the subject.

## v4.9.216 — Computed trust tier: SymPy and Pint from the forge-libs Storage bucket (Nic request 2026-11-01 5:24 AM America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** Ask could not answer arithmetic. Math questions were held unless a seed row happened to carry the answer, and seed answers are not allowed. The Python cross-check loaded SymPy from the Pyodide CDN lock and Pint from PyPI through micropip, neither pinned by the app.

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/computed_math.py` (new), `rmc_core.py`, `resonance_recall.py`, `src/py_worker.js`, `src/pycheck.js`, `src/app.js`, `sw.js`, `data/forge_libs.json` and `engine/requirements-math.txt` (new), `tools/sync_forge_libs.py`, `tools/check_216.py`, `tools/audit_204.py`, the Test and Pages workflows, and `supabase/migrations/20261101120000_forge_libs_bucket_216.sql`.

**Resolution:**
- **Grammar, no eval:** the engine's own tokenizer and recursive-descent parser handle number words and digits; plus, minus, times, divided by and over; percent of and what percent; powers (to the power of, squared, cubed, ^); square and cube roots; parentheses; solve for one unknown; and unit conversion from an explicit unit table. Ambiguous units (gallon, quart, pint, cup, fluid ounce, ounce, ton, calorie, year, month, bare degree) are held with a clarify question. Anything outside the grammar is held as MATH-UNPARSED. Division by zero, an exponent over 4096 and a result over 4000 digits are held too.
- **Computed tier:** SymPy computes (Pint with explicit units for conversions). The engine writes a FORGE-COMPUTATION-RECEIPT/v1 row (tier computed, trust trusted, seeded false, receipt id calc:sha256[:24]). It is the only source of that tier: a phone row that claims it is dropped, and a client-set `_computed` is ignored. Recall locks on the receipt for this question (`computation_receipt`, still phase-aligned). The normal admit gate decides. Echo for a math question (`computed_quantity_reparse`) re-parses the spoken sentence, checks it states the asked quantity, and recomputes the value. A math question that was not computed holds with no fallback to other rows.
- **Libraries:** seven pure-Python wheels are pinned by exact version and SHA-256 (equal to PyPI's digests): sympy 1.13.3, mpmath 1.3.0, Pint 0.24.4, flexcache 0.3, flexparser 0.4, platformdirs 4.3.6, typing_extensions 4.12.2. They are for Pyodide 0.27.7 (Python 3.12). The record is `data/forge_libs.json`, its shipped copy is `FORGE_LIBS` in `src/pycheck.js`, and the CI lock is `engine/requirements-math.txt`. The wheels are not in the shipset.
- **Phone:** when the engine understands a math question but cannot import its libraries, it holds with `needs_libs` (MATH-LIBS-NOT-LOADED). The worker then fetches the pinned wheels from the public bucket URLs without credentials, checks each with fetch integrity and a second SHA-256, unpacks it only after the match, keeps it in the Cache API (`forge-libs-v1`), and asks again. A mismatch is never installed and its cache entry is deleted. The service worker keeps `forge-libs-v1` across updates and caches the pinned Pyodide runtime (`forge-pyodide-v0.27.7`), so math works offline after first use. The cross-check now uses the same wheels; micropip, PyPI and the CDN SymPy are gone. No key is in the client.
- **Storage:** the `forge-libs` bucket was created through a migration: public read, 10 MB file limit, zip MIME types, and no `storage.objects` policies, so anon writes are refused (checked: HTTP 400 row-level security). Wheels uploaded 2026-11-01 about 6:15 AM CT with the service key from the environment (never in the repo or client); every public URL, fetched without credentials, matches its pinned SHA-256. `tools/verify_live.js` repeats this each release.
- **Superseded:** the 4.9.215 R4215 dry-run example "17 times 23 is held" still holds in the gate, where the libraries are not importable by the dry-run check (`needs_libs`). On a phone with the wheels loaded, it is now computed.
- **Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned):** 27 admitted / 33 held, 0 false admits (4.9.215: 22 / 38), run with the wheels importable. The five changes are all arithmetic: 17×23, √144, 19×21, 144÷12 and 100÷4. "How many meters are in a kilometer?" is still held because the grammar has no article-as-one ("a kilometer"); it was left alone, not tuned.
- **Registry:** R4216-COMPUTED-MATH (Partial: the server-side fallback endpoint has no math libraries, and the grammar has no article-as-one). Pinned Partial 175 → 176.
- **CI fix:** in the first push (2a5a60d) the new pip step was unquoted, and `:all: ` made both workflow files invalid YAML, so neither Test nor Deploy Pages ran. The step is now quoted, and every workflow file parses.

## v4.9.215 — Release C4: the old JS answer path is deleted; the Dry-Run tab runs the Python engine (Nic approved 2026-11-01 4:16 AM, option B 4:21 AM America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** Ask has run the Python engine (rmc_core) since 4.9.183, but the old JS answer path (`Kernel.process` / `ForgeKernel.process`, about 400 kB of kernel.js) was still shipped, the Dry-Run tab still ran it on a copy of memory, the hosted word-overlap recall (`forge_tools.recallHosted`, `pipeline_tools.recallFromBank`) was still reachable, and 235 tests plus 94 requirement links still proved behaviour of a path no user turn reaches.

**Where:** `src/kernel.js` (557,049 → about 147,000 characters: the 179 owners reachable only from `#api:process`, with roots = every other kernel API key plus the constructor statements, then the bindings left unused; nothing is called by computed name — no eval, `new Function`, `window[...]`/`root[...]` or bracket calls on the kernel/forge objects), `src/forge.js` (the `process` wrapper, `pendingBridge` and the unused `LC` helper), `src/forge_tools.js` (`recallHosted` deleted; `recall_memory` / `rmc_memory_recall` refuse `FORGE-TOOL-RETIRED`; `recallMemory`'s device-path and vector refusals stay), `src/pipeline_tools.js` (`recallFromBank` deleted; `researchExcerpt` always needs the fetch), `src/app.js` (`dryRun` rewritten; the old-path renderers `resultKind` and `traceHTML` deleted; Audit empty states), `index.html` (Dry-Run text), `src/req_packet.js` (`RETIRED_LINKS`, `RETIRED_REQUIREMENTS`), `research/math_honesty.js` (its Echo section measured the old JS Echo; now reports itself retired, no rates), `tools/c_parity.js` (trimmed), `tools/check_215.py`, `data/c4_retired_tests.json`, `verify/r4215_suite.js`, `verify/r4110/r4183/r4212/r4214_suite.js` (assertions rewritten where the old path was the evidence, names unchanged).

## v4.9.214 — Release C3: derive and reparse move to the Pāṇini wording module next to grammar.js (Nic plan, stage C3, 2026-10-31 America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** The sentence parse that the wording step and Echo rely on (`derive`, `reparseClause`, with its canonical forms, kāraka checks, economy and given-compound choice, person swap and held-reading ancestry) was a group of functions inside the `Kernel` closure, between the lexicon and the old answer engine, and shared two per-turn variables (`rsIn`, `heldNow`) with the turn loop. C4 could not delete the old engine without cutting the parser out of the wording step.

**Where:** new `src/panini_wording.js` (`KPaniniWording.Wording(ctx)`, loaded after `grammar.js` and before `kernel.js`, precached by `sw.js`), `src/kernel.js` (calls `Wording` with its lexicon view `glex`, `lex`, `featsOf`, `memNames`, `activeFacts`, `M`, `clone`, `canon`, `G` and takes back `derive`, `reparseClause`, `reparseClause0`, `content`, `swapToks`, `unknownToks`, `swap`, `economy`, `animNP`; the turn loop calls `resetTurnState()`, a resolved clarification calls `setHeld(...)`, the trace reads `rsInNow()`), `tools/c_parity.js` (10 more turns: a two-reading clarification and its answer, garden-path, object and subject questions; `data/c_parity_211.json` re-recorded from the 4.9.211 source), `verify/r4214_suite.js`; `verify/r4110_suite.js` looks for the 4.9.91 OPEN-NOMINAL marker in `panini_wording.js`, where it moved (test name unchanged).

**Resolution:** Moved verbatim with the same API (`kernel.derive`, `deriveSource`, `reparseClause`, `reparseSource`, `KKernel.parseClause`, `parseRenderedClause`, `renderClause`). The two per-turn variables now live in the module, behind three accessors, and are set at exactly the places they were set before. The lexicon's word-level `derive(w, check)` (morphology) stays in the kernel's `Lexicon`. Engine decides → Pāṇini realizes → Echo re-parses is unchanged. No behaviour change: the 30-turn parity transcript equals the 4.9.211 record byte for byte. Release gate 100% green. The old answer path is untouched and still in `kernel.js` (C4 needs Nic's approval); Ask's ε normaliser unchanged (/3). Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned): **22 admitted, 38 held, 0 false admits**, identical to 4.9.213. No LLM.

Release gate 100% green. The old answer path is untouched (C4 needs Nic's approval). Ask's ε normaliser is unchanged (/3). Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned): **22 admitted, 38 held, 0 false admits**, identical to 4.9.211. No LLM.

## v4.9.211 — Release B: DRIFT-SPEC/v1, one drift policy, ordered checks, memory write gate, server-side ε recompute (Nic plan, 2026-10-31 America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** Drift law lived in many places with different edges. The hard stop was `> 0.8` in most files, so ε = 0.80 itself was quarantined instead of stopped, and some code checked quarantine before the hard stop. The Ask gate also used an unsourced σ_res ≤ 0.35 admit gate. Forbidden transitions were checked only in some lanes. Nothing checked a rising drift trend. The Remember tap and the curators' writes went to the signed writer with no drift law on the trace, Echo was a coverage number only (a lost "not" or "if" still passed), and the server trusted the phone's numbers. Athena's 0.78 gate appeared in the papers and docs as if it were a gate.

**Where:** `src/kmath.js` (`DRIFT_POLICY`, `driftMeasure`, `policyForbidden`, `policyBackward`, `policyCurvature`, `thetaAdmit`, `coherence`, `trendRising`, `accelerationFlag`, `classifyDrift`, `evaluateChiEligibility`, `echoClasses`, `admitDecision`; `DRIFT_TABLE` elevated is now `ε < 0.80`), new `engine/rmc_engine_v1/drift_policy.py` (the Python mirror, same numbers to 6 decimals), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/mobile_config.py` (`DRIFT_POLICY`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`admit_gate` runs `classify_drift`; new `write_gate`; `memory_write.proposed` needs the write gate), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/chi_correction_gate.py` (intervene above θ_admit), the `> 0.8` → `≥ 0.80` sweep (JS: athena_doc, drift_194, geometry_199, gilligan_codex, kernel, lexicon_growth_198, mea_p, dream_idle, ede, drift_v43, drift_export, lexicon; Python: deep_dry_run_orchestrator, drift_194, geometry_199, gilin_crp_201, gilligan_anchor, language_core_engines, language_core_orchestration, lexicon_growth_198, measurement_kernel, rmc_renderer_200, world_model_v1, chi_hardening, drift_v43), `src/sb_sync.js` (`insertAdmitted` sends the write trace in the signed proof), `src/app.js` (Remember refuses a failed gate and names the rule; a held reply names the drift region; an answer that cannot be stored is tagged), `src/curators_208.js` (lanes from `classifyDrift`; Athena rejects forbidden; Gilligan's one χ re-measure must fall; write gate; chain-break halt), `supabase/migrations/20261101010000_drift_policy_211.sql` (applied: `app.drift_policy`, `app.drift_eps`, `app.phase_backward`, `app.echo_class`, `app.receipts_chain_ok`, public `drift_policy`/`drift_eps`; `app.memory_writer_insert_signed` now enforces the drift law), `supabase/functions/memory-writer/writer.js` and `index.ts` (deployed), `supabase/tests/drift_writer_211_test.sql`, `supabase/tests/writer_lockdown_205_test.sql`, Pyodide bundle + SRI, new `tools/check_211.py`, `verify/r4211_suite.js`; assertions updated for the inclusive hard stop in r4118, r4169, r4194, r4195, r4196, r4205 (the row carries a write gate), r4208 (0.79 quarantine, 0.80 hard).

**Resolution:** One policy (`profile nic-2026-10-14/v2`, the same constants in JS, Python and SQL, suite-checked). Checks run in this order and the first match wins: (1) a forbidden transition at any ε (RMC numbering: Φ5→Φ8 primary, Φ4→Φ8, and a backward step on a committed path); (2) ε ≥ 0.80 hard stop; (3) acceleration (only where timed phase events exist); (4) 0.40 ≤ ε < 0.80 quarantine, explore only; (5) 0.35 < ε < 0.40, or a drift rising over 3 consecutive steps, gets one χ-correct pass through Gilligan's guard and a re-measure; (6) clean at ε ≤ θ_admit = min(0.35, 0.4/(1 + 0.5·L)), L the circular curvature of the recent phase path. ε is never clipped; N = max(1, depth); C = 1 − ε feeds the −λD penalty. The circuit breaker stays at 3 failures, 60 s recovery (window 0.7 s, Decision 6). Athena's 0.78 gate, the router 0.72/0.78, GILIN 0.78 and the σ_res ≤ 0.35 admit gate are recorded as reference only and do not gate (σ_res is reported). The GILIN χ recovery code is not adopted. **Memory writes:** a write needs the RMC stages the answer actually passed (Φ5 measured, Φ6 clean or corrected, Φ7 named: the reply names a curated concept, Φ8 spoken; the write is step 9), the write-trace ε (metric `eps_s/rsoc-v4.8+circ/v1`, 0.119133 for an uncorrected answer) clean, Echo ≥ 0.97 (spoken answers keep 0.85), C ≥ 0.87, and the categorical checks: negation, certainty and condition words must match the source sentence the answer came from. System replies that carry "not" ("What can you do?", help) therefore cannot be remembered. **Server:** `app.drift_eps` recomputes the write-trace ε from the phase trace in the signed proof; the memory-writer Edge Function and the SQL function refuse `KB-WRITER-DRIFT-INPUT-REQUIRED`, `-EPS-MISMATCH`, `-FORBIDDEN`, `-HARD-STOP`, `-DRIFT`, `-UNNAMED`, `-ECHO`, `-COHERENCE`, and `-CHAIN-BREAK` when `app.receipts_chain_ok()` fails (a chain break also halts the curators: `CURATE-HALTED-CHAIN-BREAK`). Decisions and deviations, for Nic: (a) the Ask admission ε is still the engine's candidate measurement (`symbolic_epsilon`, normaliser n = 3, labelled `eps_s/rmc-candidate/v1`); switching it to N = max(1, depth) roughly triples ε and would stop most admits, so it is left for a decision; (b) the server compares its recomputed ε with the trace ε it was sent (`p_proof.drift.eps`), not with the candidate ε, which it cannot recompute; (c) the server's C ≥ 0.87 reads the engine's C score from the proof, because 1 − ε ≥ 0.87 would mean ε ≤ 0.13 and contradict the 0.35 admit edge; (d) Ask has no phase timings, so acceleration is not measured there; (e) `drift_v43`'s σ stays linear because its history holds MinHash values, not phases. Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned): **22 admitted, 38 held, 0 false admits**, every reply identical to 4.9.210. No LLM.

## v4.9.210 — Release A2: anchors by engine resonance, unsourced shared rows quarantined, Echo reads the question frame (Nic decisions, 2026-10-31 America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** In 4.9.209 the phone still chose which anchors the engine could see with a shipped word-to-concept index (`data/anchors_188/index.json`, `app.js askAnchorShards`): a surface phrase picked whole shards (the word "blue" loaded the Hamlet and Jupiter rows). Moving that into the engine naively matched words inside names ("MOON Jae-in" as the Moon) and lost 75 multi-word place names. Unsourced product-curator pack rows could be premises ("What is gravity?" → "tides are caused by the Moon's gravity mainly"). Echo was word coverage only: "What color is my car?" held against "My car is red." (Echo 0.5), and "Why is the ocean salty?" admitted "One of the most well known qualities of the ocean is that it is salty." while the NOAA cause sentence failed ("salty" vs "salt").

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/resonance_recall.py` (`compound_names`, `compound_id`, `row_concepts`, `question_concepts`; `rank_memory` uses them), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`preselect_pools` takes an `anchors` pool, cap 16, matched by each anchor's own curated concepts; `shared_sourced`; unsourced shared rows marked quarantined in `_rows`; `concepts` carried to the recaller), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/memory_recaller.py` (carries `concepts`), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/echo_question.py` (frame-aware Echo), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/concept_lexicon.json` (`values`: colour words are values of colour; numbers are values of age and number), `src/app.js` (`askAnchorPool` replaces `askAnchorShards`; the index is no longer read by Ask), `src/curators_208.js` (cached anchors go to the pool), `tools/live_188.py` (`anchor_pool`), `tools/audit_204.py` (the cold run uses the pool path), new `tools/check_210.py`, `verify/r4210_suite.js`; `r4206` (cache path is now `KAnchorCache.all`), `r4209` (exhausted-fallback case is now "Why is my car red?"; its cold check is pinned to its own record).

**Resolution:** (1) Every cached anchor goes to the engine as a pool; the engine keeps the anchors whose own curated packet concepts (their class) meet the question's concepts, longest compound name first ("burkina faso" → A.BURKINAFASO, generated from the words, no hand list), then ranks by trust and phase resonance as before. In running text a word inside a proper-name compound (two or more adjacent capitalised words, with name glue such as "and"/"of") does not name a general concept, so "MOON Jae-in" is A.MOONJAEIN, not A.MOON. (2) A shared row (tier confirmed_shared) is a premise only with a source URL or a trust trace resting on a trusted anchor; otherwise it is quarantined evidence, and a held question is queued for Athena as before. (3) Echo reads the question frame: a WH-attribute slot is covered by a value of that attribute from the concept lexicon ("what colour" by "red"), an adjective in -y is covered by its base noun when the base is in the answer ("salty" by "salt"), and a why-question must be answered by a sentence that states a cause (because, due to, comes from, results, …) or Echo is capped at 0.5. Live checks: "What is the Moon?" speaks a celestial Moon anchor; Burkina Faso, Bosnia and Herzegovina, Antigua and Barbuda resolve; "What is gravity?" holds (no tides line); "What color is my car?" answers "My car is red."; the ocean answer is the NOAA cause sentence. Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned): **22 admitted, 38 held, 0 false admits**; the one changed reply is the ocean (now the NOAA cause sentence). `data/anchors_188/index.json` still ships (historical tools and r4206 read it); Ask does not.

## v4.9.209 — Release A: candidate fallback, trust-first recall, no auto-write on Ask, curated contributions read back (Nic plan, 2026-10-31 America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** Live "Why is the sky blue?" held. The phone sent `sharedKnowledgeView().admitted.slice(-12)` as "confirmed shared" memory: the last 12 Z-pack rows (unsourced, trust unknown, unrelated). "Zenith marks sky overhead" became a frame-role lock that outranked the trusted NASA anchor, failed Echo (0.5), and Ask held with no second try. Every admitted Ask answer was auto-written (FORGE_ADMITTED, receipts, the shared writer), against "only explicit user requests write lasting memory". Curated `kb.contributions` were never read back (its read policy is contributor/owner only). `memory_writer_hosted.py` still called the old direct RPC path, and `rmc_core.UNSOURCED_HELD` named deleted `ho-*` ids.

**Where:** `engine/rmc_engine_v1/resonance_recall.py` (`_trusted`; a trusted concept anchor beats an unsourced role lock; trust first in both orderings), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/rmc_core.py` (`run` wraps `_run_once` with the bounded fallback; `memory_write` is a proposal with a pure `bank` decision; `UNSOURCED_HELD` and the hosted commit removed), `engine/rmc_engine_v1/memory_writer_hosted.py` (Edge Function path, same canonical args/sha as `sb_sync.insertAdmitted`), `supabase/migrations/20261101000000_contributions_read_209.sql` (applied: `public.kb_contributions_admitted`, `public.kb_contributions_digest`), new `src/contrib_cache.js`, `src/sb_sync.js`, `src/app.js` (askMemory by surface phrase; remembered rows; Remember button; "remember this" and "remember that …"; Ask path writes nothing), `src/curators_208.js` (curation body from the proposal), `index.html`, `sw.js`, Pyodide bundle + SRI, new `tools/cold60.py`, `tools/check_209.py`, `data/cold_209_results.json`, `docs/LIVE_TEST_209.md`, `verify/r4209_suite.js`; `r4185` (expects the proposal), `r4186` (writer API), `r4207` (reproduces the newest cold record; the 4.9.207 record stays as history).

**Resolution:** (1) When the top candidate fails Echo or the gate, its own rows are dropped and the same engine and gate run again, at most 3 times, in rank order; every number on a fallback answer is measured. No fallback on a hard stop, on a conflicted derive, or into research rows; the record (`ENGINE-CANDIDATE-FALLBACK` / `-EXHAUSTED` / `-NO-NEXT`) lists every try. Trusted anchors outrank unsourced rows; with all-trusted memory the order is unchanged. Shared pack rows are chosen by the question's surface phrase, not by position. (2) Ask writes nothing. The engine returns `memory_write.proposed` (committed false). "remember this" or the Remember tap keeps the admitted answer on the phone and sends it through the locked signed writer (only a linked, signed device succeeds; otherwise it stays pending on the phone). "remember that <statement>" keeps the user's statement on the phone only (never shared; questions refused; secrets go to the vault). Remembered rows are fed back to Ask and ranked by resonance. Athena's curation writes keep using the locked writer. (3) Admitted `kb.contributions` facts are read through two SECURITY DEFINER, read-only functions (granted to anon), cached in IndexedDB, refetched only when the digest changes, and kept only if admitted, in the clean band, with contributor and a trusted-anchor trust trace; they enter Ask as hosted memory under the same gate. (5) Lookup audit (Nic: "everything still looks up through phase resonance?"): `rank_memory` now ranks trust first, then phase resonance (grace = rpm weight × cos² of the phase gap, as an integer key), then the frame-role/concept packet, with length and id only as tie-breaks; `memory_recaller.recall_memory` lost its token-overlap branch (`_score_node`, `legacy_overlap`), so every caller gets resonance recall; the phone no longer matches row text at all: the shared packs and the cached contributions go to the engine as pools and `rmc_core.preselect_pools` keeps the rows whose resonance-packet concepts meet the question's (trust, then specific concepts, then id; capped 12/16). Still lexical, reported for a decision: the anchor shard index in `app.js askAnchorShards` (a shipped surface → concept map that picks which cached anchors enter the engine; moving it into the engine's concept packets admitted a wrong-sense anchor for "What is the Moon?" and drops 75 multi-word place names, so it was not switched), the Wikipedia title search (quarantined research, never a premise), and the old JS core recall (`forge_tools.recallHosted`, `pipeline_tools.recallFromBank`, `forge.js`, `kernel.js`), which no live path reaches (Release C). (4) The Python writer posts to `/functions/v1/memory-writer` with the signed proof; its canonical JSON and sha match the phone byte for byte. Cold 60 (recorded, not tuned): **22 admitted, 38 held, 0 false admits** (was 20/40); new: largest planet (via fallback), why the ocean is salty (a weak but Echo-passing line: the better NOAA sentence fails Echo coverage, an Echo limitation left as is). Known limits: "What color is my car?" after "remember that my car is red" holds (Echo 0.5: the reply does not carry "color"); "Is my car red?" admits.

## v4.9.208 — Athena and Gilligan on as curators (Nic decision (f), step 4, 2026-10-31 America/Chicago)

**Conflict:** `dream_idle` was off, so the idle mesh never ran. When it did run, its gaps were letter holes, not real questions. The Ask → mesh bridge had been dead since 4.9.183. Lookup was `semantic_overlap` recall and then Wikipedia. Measured drift was a constant (0.08 / 0.10). `measuredOf` relabelled Wikipedia sentences as `curated / allow_listed`. Athena admits never ran Echo. "Writes" went to session RAM behind a write key that accepted any 12-character string. The Custody tab said "always on" while the mesh was switched off.

**Where:** new `src/curators_208.js` (the curator logic), new `src/app_curators.js` (Custody tab, timer, toggle, phone wiring), `src/app_custody.js` (defers to the curators: the old mesh is not booted and the Wikipedia enrichment is not attached), `src/app.js` (`window.KAskAnchors`; a held question becomes a gap), `src/next_layer.js` (`dream_idle: true`), `index.html` (scripts, strip text), `sw.js`, `verify/r4208_suite.js`. The dream_idle default assertions in r4183–r4198 now expect it on (their test-only toggles restore it to on).

**Resolution:** Gaps are the questions Ask held, de-duplicated and persisted on the phone (`localStorage['forge.curation.v1']`). Athena looks each one up through the same deterministic engine Ask uses (`rmc_core`: resonance packets, measured ε, C, Echo). Memory is ONLY trusted anchors: the shipped starter set plus the Supabase anchors cached on the phone, chosen by concept as Ask does. Phone, hosted and receipt stores are empty, and there is no Wikipedia, so Wikipedia is never a premise and nothing is labelled curated. An admit needs the engine's own gate (ε ≤ 0.35, C ≥ 0.87, Echo ≥ 0.85). It then needs the **Echo re-parse**: the candidate sentence alone is the only memory, and the engine must re-derive the same sentence for the same question with Echo passing. The old JS clause grammar round-trip is recorded, but it does not gate, because it does not parse most encyclopedic sentences. Before any write, Gilligan checks that the engine's trust trace rests on one of the supplied trusted anchors (not Wikipedia), that ε is numeric and ≤ 0.35, and that the gate was met. The 0.35–0.40 band gets one re-measure after an anchor refresh, with no invented correction. 0.40–0.80 stays in quarantine until the anchor digest changes. The hard band is kept cold. Bands come from `KMath.driftGovernance`, unchanged. An admitted row is written to Supabase only through `KSb.insertAdmitted`, the locked-down writer from 4.9.205 (linked account, writer role, verified device signature). Otherwise it stays on the phone as PENDING, is retried every 5 minutes through the same writer, and is not fed to Ask. Ticks run only while the page is open and visible, never while Ask is answering, at most one gap every 15 s. The Custody tab text describes exactly this and has a "Curators on" toggle. With the real engine and the starter + g190 anchors: "Why is the sky blue?" and "What is photosynthesis?" are admitted and the re-parse passes (pending with no session). "What is the largest planet?", "Who wrote Hamlet?" and "What is 17 times 23?" stay queued (`ATHENA-NO-ADMIT`). Open: an admitted curated row written to Supabase is not yet read back into Ask, because Ask does not read `kb.contributions`. Drift bands are unchanged. No LLM.
