## v4.9.118 — MATH HONESTY OFFLINE RESEARCH (Nic 2026-10-27; queue item 3)
- Design sources: RMC Addendum §3 and §8–§9 (executable is not calibrated and is not empirically validated); RSOC chapter 4; the architecture Page “Later — mathematical honesty program”; 4.9.109 MathContractRegistry and WM Spec Hardening I.1. This is an offline research harness. It is not a Mobile numerical manuscript claim. No new answer. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. Shared KB stays empty. No language model.
- (1) Drift divisors the code already distinguishes. `kmath.driftFunctional` divides by ρ = max(1, depth) (`drift-measure/addendum-3/v1`). `ede.js`, `evolve.js`, and `reason.js` stamp n = 3 and divide by 3. RSOC n = max(1, ρ+1) is noted in those files and is not executed: status `SOURCE_NOT_VERIFIED`. On one recorded numerator, ρ and n = 3 are not the same number and would not fall in the same Nic band. That contrast is research-only. It is not applied to the write law. The two divisors are not a manuscript equivalence.
- (2) Phase ablation stubs. Nine stubs name Φ1–Φ9. Each is unmeasured, status `stub`, and the phase scheme stays a reconstruction. No delta is reported. Φ1–Φ9 is not proven cognition.
- (3) Echo false-positive and false-negative rates on recorded Language Core surfaces. The surfaces are sentences already in the verification corpus, with the expected pass or fail recorded beside them. The rates are research-only and `SOURCE_NOT_VERIFIED` as a manuscript calibration. They are not product truth in VERSION or the README.
- (4) Mobile write law unchanged. Project and write stay at or under 0.35. χ correction stays 0.35–0.40. Quarantine stays 0.40–0.80. A hard stop stays above 0.80. Paper bands 0.40/0.80 and Addendum figures 0.50/0.78/0.80 are not adopted. ROC ≡ CFBC ≡ RSOC stays `SOURCE_NOT_VERIFIED` (I.1). System Status says the harness exists and refuses manuscript equivalence.
- (5) The harness lives in `source/research/math_honesty.js` and `source/tools/math_honesty.js`. It is not a script in the single-file app. `node tools/math_honesty.js` prints the JSON. `--md` prints the note. `--write <dir>` writes both artifacts. `FORGE-REQ-PACKET/v1` names `R4118-NONEQ` for this non-equivalence.
- Dream/SPC was not started. Creative writing was not started. Out of scope, and do not start them in this release: dream/SPC, creative writing, lexicon growth, Genesis, HCS, marketplace, agents, a shared-KB write, fluency, Soft Echo, and any language model.

## v4.9.119 — DREAM / SPC CLEANUP (Nic 2026-10-27; queue item 4, final before creative writing)
- Design sources: KERNEL_COMPONENTS containment and the 4.9.106 inspect release. This closes the leftover honesty holes. No seventh route. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. Shared KB stays empty. No language model. Nic's bands stay: project and write at or under 0.35, χ correction 0.35–0.40, quarantine 0.40–0.80, hard stop above 0.80.
- (1) Resurrection is a proposal. `kernel.resurrect` and the Memory-screen Resurrect control run the seven gates and stage a proposal. They do not write memory, the world head, or the shared KB, and they do not spend an attempt. “resurrect …” and “bring back …” keep the 4.9.106 sentence and stage the same proposal after Echo. `commitResurrection(id, true)` is the only store. A commit without that proposal is `RS-PROPOSAL-REQUIRED`. A false tap counts an attempt and stores nothing. A record whose ε_s is above 0.35 is `RS-DRIFT-NOT-PROJECTABLE`.
- (2) `must_archive` is a route input. After the circuit breaker and ghost, and before dream, `must_archive` selects `spc_cold_storage_required` with rule `CR-MUST-ARCHIVE`. The switch `mustArchive` defaults to off. When it is on, inspect keeps its list and names `must_archive`. A write or a dream on that policy is not stored. Ghost and the circuit breaker still win.
- (3) No silent second write. A dream, a resurrection phrase, a resurrection proposal, and a quarantine inspect do not write memory, move the world head, or fill the shared KB. The proposal turn may not write memory (`CR-SPC-PROPOSAL`).
- hardware SPC is not claimed. ChristPing is not built, and a resurrection does not restore without a user act. Cold storage is a software tier. Creative writing was not started. Out of scope, and do not start them in this release: creative writing, novel-idea generation, lexicon growth, math harness expansion, Genesis, HCS, marketplace, agents, a shared-KB write, fluency, Soft Echo, and any language model.
- Tests: `verify/r4119_suite.js`. Targeted gate adds that suite. HELDOUT untouched. `kernel-app.html` omits the registry note and wave field so the raw file stays within 1.920 MB. The claim table still shows id, name, status, and proof. The site keeps the full registry.

## v4.9.120 — CREATE / EXPLORE (Nic 2026-10-27; first creative-writing release)
- Design sources: the 4.9.103 intent set, EDE Algorithm 3, and the MEA-P screen. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. Shared KB stays empty. No language model. Nic's bands stay: project and write at or under 0.35, χ correction 0.35–0.40, quarantine 0.40–0.80, hard stop above 0.80. Φ1–Φ9 is not proven cognition.
- (1) Intents. CREATE and EXPLORE extend the 4.9.103 set. “invent a hypothesis”, “come up with an idea”, and “create an idea” are CREATE. “explore alternatives” is EXPLORE. “explore ideas” stays PROPOSE_EVIDENCE.
- (2) Novelty and drift. `creativeScreen` reads an EDE result. Every candidate carries N and ε_s. No memory anchor means novelty class `unbound` and claim status `rejected`. That is not a discovery claim. A projectable bounded candidate may be spoken as `hypothesis`. CREATE speaks at most one. EXPLORE speaks at most four.
- (3) Quarantine speech. An anchored candidate in the explore band may be spoken as `hypothesis` when novelty is bounded, otherwise as `speculative_branch`. A correction-band candidate is not spoken (χ only). Drift above 0.80 is `drift_hard` and is not spoken. A seal phrase with no proposal is `commit_without_proposal`. Those classes sit on `packet.creative`. The receipt type stays the existing gate refusal. Nothing is written to memory, the world head, the shared KB, the drift archive, or EDE calibration on the propose turn.
- (4) Tap. `commitIdea(id, true)` is the only commit. It sets the idea row to `committed` only when novelty is bounded and drift is projectable (ε_s ≤ 0.35). The tap does not write memory, the world head, or the shared KB. A false tap is `PERM-NEEDS-USER-TAP`. A missing proposal is `CREATIVE-PROPOSAL-REQUIRED`. A quarantine tap is `CREATIVE-DRIFT-NOT-PROJECTABLE`.
- COMPOSE (4.9.121) was not started. Suppose story branches (4.9.122) were not started. Do not start them in this release. Out of scope: a new MEA operator rewrite, lexicon growth, an LLM, Soft Echo, a shared-KB write, hardware SPC, ChristPing, and EchoForge as authority.
- Tests: `verify/r4120_suite.js`, written failing first. Targeted gate adds that suite. HELDOUT untouched.
- Single-file size. `kernel-app.html` drops spaces that sit beside `{}()[],;` after the existing comment strip, so the raw file stays within 1.920 MB. Newlines stay. Strings, templates, and regex literals stay. The Pages site keeps the spaced source. The claim table still shows id, name, status, and proof.

## v4.9.121 — COMPOSE (Nic 2026-10-27; second creative-writing release)
- Design sources: the 4.9.103 intent set and the 4.9.120 idea record. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. Shared KB stays empty. No language model. Nic's bands stay: project and write at or under 0.35, χ correction 0.35–0.40, quarantine 0.40–0.80, hard stop above 0.80.
- (1) Intent. COMPOSE extends the 4.9.103 set. “compose that idea”, “compose that meaning”, “write it out”, “write the idea out”, and “explain it as a short paragraph” are COMPOSE. “explain the idea” without that paragraph frame stays EXPLAIN. “explain the proposals” stays PROPOSE_EVIDENCE. “invent a hypothesis” stays CREATE. “explore alternatives” stays EXPLORE. “explore ideas” stays PROPOSE_EVIDENCE. “Suppose” stays SIMULATE.
- (2) Prose. The paragraph is several Language Core sentences. It names the source claim as a mention and names the claim status. It exact-Echoes. It is not Soft Echo. An empty claim is `language_unexpressible` and still speaks a refusal that exact-Echoes. A surface that fails Echo is blocked, the receipt is `echo_fail`, and the trace records `LANGUAGE_UNEXPRESSIBLE`.
- (3) Custody. Compose reads an admitted idea record or the latest spoken MSM. It does not invent a world fact. A hypothesis stays a hypothesis. A speculative branch stays a speculative branch. A stored meaning stays stored. Unbound novelty is not spoken as a discovery. Drift above 0.80 is `drift_hard` and the claim is not narrated. A compose with no selected meaning is `commit_without_proposal`. Nothing is written to memory, the world head, the shared KB, the drift archive, or EDE calibration. A later tap still commits the idea record only when drift is projectable (ε_s ≤ 0.35).
- Suppose story branches (4.9.122) were not started. Do not start Suppose in this release. Out of scope: a new MEA operator rewrite, lexicon growth as the work, an LLM, Soft Echo, a shared-KB write, hardware SPC, ChristPing, and EchoForge as authority.

## v4.9.122 — Suppose SIMULATE (Nic 2026-10-27; final locked creative release)
- Design sources: the 4.9.101 Suppose branch and the 4.9.106 dream quarantine. No new story engine. No `suppose.js`. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. Shared KB stays empty. No language model. Nic's bands stay: project and write at or under 0.35, χ correction 0.35–0.40, quarantine 0.40–0.80, hard stop above 0.80.
- (1) Isolation. “suppose a story”, “open a simulation”, “open a counterfactual”, and “what if a story” are SIMULATE. The branch id stays on the turn. It is not written to memory, the world head, the shared KB, the drift archive, or EDE calibration. A syllogism Suppose stays the 4.9.101 branch reply. A bare Suppose stays dream quarantine. A dynamics what-if stays world.dynamics.
- (2) Surface. The reply names the branch a simulation and a counterfactual. The claim status is `speculative_branch`. It is not a stored fact and not a verified claim. The turn packet stays `FORGE-TURN-PACKET/v1` at packet version 4.9.111 and exports the branch id and the containment route.
- (3) Exit. Park or end uses dream quarantine and does not write the world. A later continue with no open branch is `commit_without_proposal`. Writing, storing, remembering, committing, or sealing an open branch is `world_write`. Speaking an unbound novelty as a discovery is `novelty_unbound`. Those refusals keep the existing gate-refusal receipt. An Echo miss blocks the reply and stores nothing.
- creative queue 120–122 complete. Await Nic. Do not start a new creative phase. Out of scope: Genesis, HCS, marketplace, agents, fluency, Soft Echo, a shared-KB write, and any language model.

A compose with no selection is `commit_without_proposal`. Those refusals do not expand. An Echo miss stores nothing.

- Design sources: the 4.9.120 CREATE screen and the 4.9.123 COMPOSE anchors. No shared store. No new drift threshold. Echo stays an exact Language Core reparse. χ stays the correction function only. - (2) Held for tap. A contested or quarantine sentence is not an anchor until the person taps Admit. CREATE and COMPOSE use admitted import anchors ahead of the starter pack. Remember stays person-authored and is not cleared. Starter knowledge is not cleared unless the person asks.
- (3) Refuse. A pack that is not JSON, has the wrong schema, is empty, does not parse, lacks evidence, has an unknown standing, repeats a sentence, or asks for a shared write is refused with a typed receipt. Nothing is admitted. CREATE, COMPOSE, and SIMULATE do not write confirmed facts.

## v4.9.127 — gated confirmed_shared + letter packs A–Z (Nic 2026-10-27)
- Design sources: BRIEF_4.9.127_CONFIRMED_SHARED; curated drafts under kernel-papers/oct25/confirmed_shared_packs/ (letter_a…letter_z); README distributed confirmed path. Echo stays exact. Soft Echo stays parked. Scrapbook shared_knowledge stays empty. No language model. Soft budget stays ≤1.920 MB / ≤570 kB gzip; packs stay hosted, not inside single-file gzip.

First open / Refresh shared confirmed admits every indexed letter. Offline keeps the last-good cache. CREATE, COMPOSE, and SIMULATE never auto-write confirmed_shared.
- (3) Anchor priority. Invent and compose prefer user memory, then confirmed_shared, then import_pack, then starter_pack. Speech names the confirmed pack. Audit census shows confirmed_shared. UI never says shared KB.
- A scrapbook shared-knowledge write was not started and stays next. Out of scope: Genesis, HCS, marketplace, agents, fluency, Soft Echo, a deep IA rewrite, hardware SPC, ChristPing, EchoForge as authority.
- Tests: `verify/r4127_suite.js`, written failing first. Targeted gate adds that suite. HELDOUT untouched.

## v4.9.128 — hosted shared knowledge A–Z (Nic t117–t121, 2026-10-27)
- Design sources: BRIEF_4.9.128_SHARED_KB_AZ; BRIEF_4.9.128_PACK_REFRESH; thickened drafts (2622 curated). Echo stays exact. Soft Echo stays parked. No language model. Soft budget ≤1.920 MB / ≤570 kB gzip; packs stay hosted, not inside single-file gzip. Starter_pack / phone Remember stay user-owned and are not enlarged as the product brain.
- (1) Hosted source of truth. Full A–Z under `packs/confirmed_shared/{a…z,index}.json` (index 4.9.128; ≥1890 admitted / 2622 curated). Product shared knowledge for everyone is this hosted layer — not scrapbook, not starter, not a mass localStorage/IndexedDB dump.
- (2) Read for all. App `pullHostedSharedPack` / `ensureSharedKnowledge` fetches packs into session RAM for invent/compose anchors. Phone `confirmed_shared` store is wiped/kept empty on pull. Scrapbook `shared_knowledge` stays empty.
- (3) Write gated by Nic's key. `writeSharedKnowledge` / `admitConfirmedShared` refuse with `SHARED-KB-WRITE-KEY-REQUIRED` unless `setSharedKbWriteKey` + matching `writeKey`. Product-curator boolean alone is not enough. Default users are read-only.
- (4) Naming. UI card and Audit say **Shared knowledge base** (hosted · read for everyone · write needs Nic's key · not phone DB). Invent/compose speech says “the shared knowledge base”. Pack provenance id remains `confirmed_shared`.
- (5) CREATE/COMPOSE/SIMULATE never auto-write shared knowledge. User memory still wins over hosted anchors.
- Tests: `verify/r4128_suite.js` lead targeted suite; `r4127_suite.js` softened. HELDOUT untouched.

## v4.9.162 — READ-path connect for hosted shared knowledge (Nic, 2026-10-28)
- Design sources: Nic pt124u (“connect all the damn paths”); BRIEF_4.9.128_SHARED_KB_AZ; CONFLICTS 4.9.128–4.9.161. Echo stays exact LC. Soft Echo stays parked. No language model. Soft budget unchanged; packs stay hosted (index 4.9.161 counts kept — this ship is routing, not corpus thicken).
- (1) READ-path connect. Ordinary factual WH / yes-no / about consult **memory → hosted confirmed_shared (session pull) → GKS → else AQ-1 / research.web**. Hosted facts already fed invent/compose; they now also answer recall before research. Surface-aware `aboutSubjMatch` (propn ↔ adj+head, PP peel) so “First Amendment” meets pack subjects without adj-blind `entKey` drowning. Provenance via `confirmedTold` / Shared knowledge base; Echo strips the derive-only tag.
- (2) Locks unchanged: read for all; write-key; no phone-DB mass write; CREATE/COMPOSE/SIMULATE never auto-write; scrapbook empty; lookup never stores into shared KB.
- Tests: `verify/r4162_suite.js` lead (First Amendment from shared KB; deliberate miss → AQ-1; YN + memory-wins); `r4128_suite.js` still in gate.
- Soft single-file budget stays ≤1.920 MB / ≤570 kB gzip (Nic: size never blocks). Soft gzip leave under 570 kB may sit under the historical ~5 kB preference after the READ connect; hard gate unchanged.

## v4.9.163 — civics WH substance + CS discourse continuity (Nic, 2026-10-28)
- Design sources: Nic pt125u (Fifth Amendment thin answer + people/person hold + follow-up lost into research.web); CONFLICTS 4.9.162 READ-path; BRIEF_4.9.128_SHARED_KB_AZ. Echo stays exact LC. Soft Echo stays parked. No language model. Soft budget unchanged; packs stay hosted.
- (1) Pathway, not a Fifth-only hack. `G-IRRN-SURFACE` collapses question readings that describe identically and differ only by irregular plural lemma (people↔person) so a clear civics WH is not held for clarify. `CS-COMPOSE-PRESENCE-ABOUT` composes a lone be-presence hit with about-facts for the same NP (Bill of Rights + rights). `CS-RECALL-RIGHTS-ABOUT` answers protect/give/grant/… WH and discourse “what does it say that it gives” from about-facts for the resolved subject before recall_none / AQ-1. Deliberate miss still AQ-1.
- (2) Pack thicken (First/Fifth). `packs/confirmed_shared/f.json` (+ site mirror): Fifth gains due process, double-jeopardy (forbid second trial), grand jury, private property, just compensation; self-incrimination rewritten to karma form; First gains press, assembly, petition. Index 4.9.163; f 262 / 271; totals 6518 / 6756. Locks unchanged: read for all; write-key; no phone-DB mass write; CREATE/COMPOSE/SIMULATE never auto-write; scrapbook empty.
- Tests: `verify/r4163_suite.js` lead (Fifth what-is substance; rights WH without people/person hold; discourse follow-up stays on CS entity; First rights + deliberate miss); `r4162_suite.js` still in gate.
- Soft single-file budget stays ≤1.920 MB / ≤570 kB gzip (Nic: size never blocks).

## v4.9.165 — RPMC→RSOC→Panini answer path (Nic t129u–t132u, 2026-10-28)
- Design sources: Nic binding — RPMC comes up with answers. Order: archives/sources → RPMC (phase-aligned recall/combine, grace, χ, echo) → RSOC select → Panini natural language. Zero canned. Silence only for no-basis novelty. Continuous fill PAUSED. No CloudAgent. Soft gzip leave ≥5 kB under 570 kB. Soft Echo parked. No LLM. Pathway not patches. Drift bands unchanged. Hosted CS read-everyone write-key. Packs stay hosted (index still 4.9.163).
- (1) Removed wrong-shape `CS-COMPOSE-CLASS-ABOUT` / class-heuristic dump and the interim CS-RESONATE-ANCESTOR answer emit. FIND helpers collect related hosted CS as **candidates only**.
- (2) `rpmcAncestorCandidates` / `graceOverAncestors` (Axiom 4 + §3.4; G_MIN fail-closed) phase-gate those candidates; optional χ; rpmWeight ranks after gate. Pathway `RPMC-ANCESTOR-CANDIDATES` (or `RPMC-ANCESTOR-NONE`) — **does not answer**.
- (3) Exact miss on factual WH falls through to AUTH-AQ1 / research.web with `rpmc_priors` staged (`RSOC-COMPOSE-EVIDENCE`). forge.js seeds priors into the research trace; archives+web gather; existing RSOC/QC select + Panini derive render natural language. First/Fifth exact CS paths unchanged. Deliberate novelty miss still AQ-1 without false CS answer.
- (4) Answer-path grace is **no longer a stub** for this gate (`math_honesty.json` `answer_path_grace.status=wired`). Soft budget unchanged.
- Tests: `verify/r4165_suite.js` lead (RPMC candidates + AQ-1; no class dump; no canned Second string; First/Fifth exact; miss AQ-1); version/registry/NEXT_BUILD/README/targeted/req_packet assert 4.9.165; packs index remains 4.9.163.

## v4.9.166 — QuotaExceeded-safe kernel.trace + AQ-1 RPMC gather (Nic, 2026-10-28)
- Design sources: live Forge Mobile 4.9.165 red banner `QuotaExceededError` on `kernel.trace` setItem while Ask had `What is h2s?`; standing laws Grok Bot computer ONLY, no CloudAgent, no canned/memory/pack hacks per question, RPMC→RSOC→Panini for factual misses, continuous fill PAUSED, soft gzip leave ≥5 kB under 570 kB.
- (1) Root cause: `MemoryStore.save` → `localStorage.setItem` with no quota handling; fat `kernel.trace` (even after `tr.slice(-200)`) throws uncaught → `app_lab.js` errBanner and can abort the turn.
- (2) Pathway fix (not one-question): `MemoryStore.save` catches QuotaExceeded, evicts soft cats (`ui_errors`/`held_writes`/…), shrinks/compacts older traces, retries; soft cats / arrays return `{ok:false,rule:'STORE-QUOTA-SOFT'}` instead of throwing. `finish()` and forge.js trace writes try/catch so the answer path completes even if trace shrinks.
- (3) Factual miss that world-model undetermined skips language-core `answer()` still runs RPMC gather at AUTH-AQ1 (`RPMC-ANCESTOR-NONE` / `CANDIDATES`) so chemical what-is (`What is h2s?`) logs the lawful pipeline — never a canned pack row, no H2S pack thicken.
- Tests: `verify/r4166_suite.js` lead (quota sim never throws; h2s/hydrogen sulfide → RPMC + AQ-1, not canned); version/registry/NEXT_BUILD/README/targeted/req_packet assert 4.9.166; packs index remains 4.9.163.

## v4.9.167 — public research vault-optional + RPMC domain ancestors (Nic, 2026-10-28)
- Design sources: live 4.9.166 H2S probe still vault/premise (`RPMC-ANCESTOR-NONE` + `AUTH-AQ1` → passphrase) despite world knowledge in archives/hosted CS; standing law archives/sources → RPMC (grace/χ/echo) → RSOC → Panini NL; zero canned; silence only for no-basis novelty; well-documented chemistry/civics must Name; Grok Bot computer ONLY; continuous fill PAUSED; soft gzip leave ≥5 kB under 570 kB.
- (1) Root cause triple: CHEM-FORMULA case-sensitive (lowercase `h2s` missed); RPMC ancestors only ordinal+class FIND (chemical/general factual → NONE); AQ-1 / `research.web` required Identity Vault unlock (`network_read+vault_write` + PERM-VAULT-LOCKED) so ordinary public science what-is demanded a passphrase.
- (2) Pathway fix (not an H2S pack): case-insensitive formula parse + bare WH-COP subject → chemistry domains/entity; `ancestorDomainCandidates` / `ARCH_DOMAIN_PRIORS` fall-through when class FIND empty (archive catalog stubs + domain-surface CS); AQ-1 always starts `research.web` without `lookupHint`; `research.web` effect `network_read` only; governance vault gate only when effect has `vault_write`; forge runs research vault-optional (seal when unlocked, else ephemeral on-device). Vault passphrase remains for true private/memory-write / capture.page / notes.seal.
- (3) Archives Naming for formula→compound: PubChem prefers the definitional lead that Names the resolved title (and keeps formula for key match); `relevant()` counts aliases toward A-PRIMARY-ALL; Wikipedia accepts chemistry formula search hits via redirecttitle/snippet, survives a missing formula-token title 404, and stamps formula aliases so subjectDrift/RSOC can project — then Panini Names. No molecule pack row.
- Tests: `verify/r4167_suite.js` lead (h2s / hydrogen sulfide / non-civics factual → RPMC-ANCESTOR-CANDIDATES + RSOC-COMPOSE-EVIDENCE + Named research without AUTH vault; Second still math-compose; authorize locked allow for research.web); w34 AQ1/CRAWLER updated for vault-optional research; packs index remains 4.9.163.
